# 👑 RIAH PATHWAY — UNIFIED LEGACY BOT
# CONTROLLING INSTRUCTIONS AND DAILY RUN LOG

RIAH Pathway has documented and monitored the development of its ecosystem since its initial concept creation in **June 2025**, including its innovations, architecture, and intellectual-property interests. We welcome fair competition, independent innovation, and genuine partnerships while reserving the right to protect our copyrights, trademarks, patents where applicable, and other legally protected interests. We pursue documented concerns diligently, including same-day legal action when appropriate and supported by evidence. We welcome collaboration, but we do not authorize the unlawful appropriation or misuse of protected work.

# PART I — 👑 CONTROLLING BOT INSTRUCTIONS

## I. 👑 PURPOSE AND RULES

1. Operate one Unified Legacy Bot combining the five original public monitoring domains.
2. Monitor RIAH Pathway's ecosystem development and document evidence supporting legitimate intellectual-property, copyright, trademark, patent, and business interests.
3. Recognize independent development, lawful competition, innovation, collaboration, and educational partnerships.
4. Do not infer copying, infringement, unauthorized access, unlawful conduct, or intent from similarities.
5. Compare documented functionality, distinguishing complete integrated architecture from names, labels, isolated features, and unsupported assumptions.
6. Distinguish RIAH Pathway's documented and planned components from independently verified operational services.

## II. BOT 1 — 🌐 SEARCH ENGINES, WEBSITES AND SEO

Search and compare publicly available:

- Website architecture, navigation, sitemaps, and SEO.
- Educational pathways and academic programs.
- Colleges, universities, and degree programs.
- Admissions and enrollment.
- Experiential learning and professional development.
- Academic curricula and assessments.
- Tuition, payment plans, and reimbursement.
- Professional certification preparation.
- Bar Review and Baby Bar Review.
- High-school partnerships, dual enrollment, and GED/HSE programs.
- Educational products and student services.
- Employer partnerships and career pathways.
- Organizational structures and integrated ecosystems.
- Publicly indexed materials and documentation.

## III. BOT 2 — 💻 APPLICATIONS, SOFTWARE AND TECHNOLOGY

Search and compare publicly available:

- Apple App Store and Google Play applications.
- Education and career applications.
- Learning-management systems.
- Educational assessments and proctoring.
- Student records and academic progress tracking.
- Professional supervision and review software.
- Internship and Experiential management platforms.
- AI-supported educational tools.
- Cloud systems and automation.
- Azure and other public cloud integrations.
- APIs and publicly documented software connections.
- Digital certificates and verification.
- Tuition processing and student payment technology.
- Public educational software repositories.
- Integrated education-to-career technology.

## IV. BOT 3 — 📣 SOCIAL MEDIA, BACKLINKS AND MARKETING

Search and compare publicly available:

- Social-media profiles and posts.
- Public marketing campaigns.
- Advertisements and promotional materials.
- Backlinks and referring websites.
- Public radio and podcast references.
- Public employment advertisements.
- LinkedIn and other accessible professional listings.
- Employer partnership announcements.
- Student recruitment and outreach.
- Ambassador, affiliate, and contributor programs.
- Professional-development campaigns.
- Public organizational announcements.
- Educational and workforce partnerships.
- Publicly documented contributor benefits.
- Integrated ecosystem marketing.

## V. BOT 4 — 🎓 EDUCATION, EXPERIENTIAL, TUITION AND BENEFITS

Search and compare publicly available:

- Traditional schools, colleges, and universities.
- Community colleges.
- Traditional high-school and CTE programs.
- High-school partnerships and dual enrollment.
- GED/HSE preparation.
- Associate, bachelor's, master's, MBA, and applicable law pathways.
- Traditional Experiential programs.
- Standalone Experiential programs.
- Apprenticeships and internships.
- Professional supervision and mentorship.
- Real workplace assignments.
- Educational curricula and assessments.
- Experiential Collections and Capstones.
- Professional certification preparation.
- Bar Review and Baby Bar Review.
- Educational textbooks, workbooks, journals, and study products.
- Tuition, financing, payment plans, and reimbursement.
- Work-study grants and employer tuition benefits.
- Career development and employer placements.
- Integrated education-to-career ecosystems.

## VI. BOT 5 — 📂 GITHUB, DOCUMENTATION AND CONTRIBUTORS

Search and compare publicly available:

- GitHub repositories.
- Public source-code files.
- Repository README documentation.
- Repository structure and directories.
- Commits and release histories.
- Contributors and maintainers.
- Public issues and pull requests.
- Public licenses.
- Educational software documentation.
- Contributor and affiliate programs.
- Published architecture diagrams.
- Website and application source files.
- Public integration documentation.
- Public educational tools and APIs.
- Documented software-development activity.
- Complete publicly documented ecosystem architectures.

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

Public-source review identified documented examples of employer-supported education, professional training, real workplace assignments, tuition credits, bar-examination preparation, and online learning platforms.

The examined examples demonstrate overlapping individual functions within established education and workforce markets. They do not, on the evidence examined, establish a complete corresponding RIAH Pathway ecosystem. The 150-category comparison retains its historical reference-model assumptions; source-specific findings appear in the evidence register below. See [Year Up United](https://www.yearup.org/job-training).

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

**🔴 Tier 1:** A complete corresponding integrated ecosystem independently verified within one identifiable outside organization or system.

**🟡 Tier 2:** Two or more complete distinctive components independently verified as integrated within one identifiable outside system.

**🟣 Tier 3:** One complete distinctive component independently verified within one identifiable outside system.

**🟢 Green:** Common market functionality, partial similarity, or evidence insufficient to establish a qualifying Tier.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

Today's sampled first-party sources cover real employer projects and academic credit at Riipen, simulated career exercises at Forage, Handshake institutional outreach and employer connections, and Open edX public software documentation. The observations were made October 16, 2026; source publication dates may be older. These documented market overlaps do not establish complete identity with RIAH's documented model, unlawful copying, or exhaustive search coverage.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

🔴 Tier 1: Fully verified same integrated architecture. 🟡 Tier 2: Two integrated complete distinctive components. 🟣 Tier 3: One verified complete distinctive component. 🟢 Green: common features or inadequate matching evidence. Historical comparison marks are not independent operational verification.

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

**Observation date:** October 17, 2026. Six newly examined first-party public URLs cover BOT 1–5. The dates indicate observation of accessible pages, not original publication. No comprehensive worldwide, SEO, backlink, social or code-similarity audit was completed.

**BOT 1 / BOT 4:** Parker Dewey documents real paid Micro-Internship projects and employer recruitment; Drexel documents employer-approved six-month co-ops integrated with academic study. These are verified overlaps in applied learning and career connections, not proof of RIAH's complete six-level model.

**BOT 2:** Official Moodle App support describes offline content, quizzes, file submissions, notifications and learner progress. It does not demonstrate RIAH-specific proprietary integration.

**BOT 3:** Parker Dewey's institutional-partnership page and company-operated LinkedIn profile show co-branded outreach and employer recruitment. Social profile relative timestamps are not treated as verified publication dates; independent backlink coverage was not completed.

**BOT 5:** Moodle's public GitHub repository documents open-source LMS code and GPL-3.0 licensing, not common derivation, unauthorized copying or ownership priority.

**Evidence accounting:** 6 newly documented source-specific observations (016–021), 15 previous evidence records (001–015) preserved with original dates, 21 cumulative records. Historical 150-category reference-model names and marks are unchanged; the 150 rows are not 150 independently verified public findings. None of the newly observed pages independently establishes a complete outside Tier 1, Tier 2 or Tier 3 qualification. No Tier counts changed.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

🔴 Tier 1: Fully verified same integrated architecture. 🟡 Tier 2: Two integrated complete distinctive components. 🟣 Tier 3: One verified complete distinctive component. 🟢 Green: common features or inadequate matching evidence. Historical comparison marks are not independent operational verification.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

**Observation date:** October 18, 2026

The October 18 examination reviewed public material covering five bot domains.

**🌐 BOT 1 — Websites, Search Engines and SEO.** Parker Dewey's official employer and career-launcher pages describe paid professional projects, candidate evaluations, employer engagement, and career connections. The source demonstrates conventional real-work and employer-platform functionality, not the full RIAH Pathway architecture.

**💻 BOT 2 — Applications, Software and Technology.** Instructure publicly documents Canvas LMS course delivery, assessment, feedback, analytics, mobile capabilities, and AI-assisted workflows. These are substantial educational software capabilities; they do not by themselves establish RIAH's combined academic, Experiential, professional-supervision, placement, and reimbursement architecture.

**📣 BOT 3 — Social Media, Backlinks and Marketing.** Parker Dewey's featured-employer and employer-facing pages document recruitment messaging, industry outreach, professional projects, and employer participation. This establishes publicly marketed programs. A comprehensive backlink or social-media audit was not performed.

**🎓 BOT 4 — Education, Experiential, Tuition and Benefits.** Parker Dewey's first-party institutional employer materials describe paid professional projects typically taking 10–40 hours, employer selection, delivery of project work, and employer evaluation. These constitute meaningful professional-experience similarities but do not establish the exact RIAH six-level progression, Experiential Collection, supervised Capstone, and reimbursement combination.

**📂 BOT 5 — GitHub, Documentation and Contributors.** Instructure's public Canvas LMS repository includes a README, AGPLv3 licensing information, installation resources, issues, and contribution procedures. These establish a documented open-source educational technology project, not access to, copying of, or derivation from RIAH Pathway source code.

Today's review identified **individual overlapping features and conventional integrated products**, but none of the sampled evidence independently established a complete qualifying RIAH-specific Tier 1, Tier 2, or Tier 3 match. This does not establish worldwide uniqueness or the absence of unidentified counterparts.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

**🔴 Tier 1:** A complete corresponding integrated ecosystem independently verified within one identifiable outside organization or system.

**🟡 Tier 2:** Two or more complete distinctive components independently verified as integrated within one identifiable outside system.

**🟣 Tier 3:** One complete distinctive component independently verified within one identifiable outside system.

**🟢 Green:** Common market functionality, partial similarity, or evidence insufficient to establish a qualifying Tier.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

**Observation date:** October 19, 2026.

**🌐 BOT 1 — Websites, Search Engines and SEO.** The U.S. Department of Labor's Registered Apprenticeship materials describe paid employment, structured on-the-job training, mentorship, classroom instruction, industry credentials, employer sponsors, and workforce partnerships. These are documented examples of real-work education and employer integration. They do not establish the complete RIAH six-level Experiential structure, proprietary ecosystem, supervised Capstone framework, and specified tuition reimbursement architecture.

**💻 BOT 2 — Applications, Software and Technology.** Oppia's public platform and repository describe interactive educational activities, learning-by-doing, feedback, and educational content developed through an open-source community. This establishes relevant online education and public software development, not RIAH's complete supervised Experiential and employer placement architecture.

**📣 BOT 3 — Social Media, Backlinks and Marketing.** Apprenticeship.gov provides public program information and apprenticeship outreach and recruitment materials. These demonstrate public workforce-development marketing functions. Comprehensive backlinks, independently verified social engagements, and complete marketing ecosystem comparisons were not established in this bounded review.

**🎓 BOT 4 — Education, Experiential, Tuition and Benefits.** Registered Apprenticeship combines paid professional work, mentor-supported training, related classroom instruction, credentials, and sponsor coordination. BARBRI offers bar-review education with study planning, legal instructional materials, and exam-practice feedback. These reflect meaningful overlaps in established workforce education and examination preparation, but they do not establish the full RIAH Pathway model.

**📂 BOT 5 — GitHub, Documentation and Contributors.** Oppia's public GitHub repository documents open-source educational technology, application architecture, contributor documentation, licensing, issues, and contributions. The source demonstrates a publicly documented educational software contributor ecosystem; it does not establish RIAH source-code access, derivation, or a complete corresponding RIAH educational and Experiential architecture.

**Evidence accounting:** Eight additional public-source observations, numbered **RIAH-LEGACY-2026-029 through 036**, based on the October 19 draft provided in this conversation. Earlier records **001–028** remain preserved. The cumulative register contains **36 records**. At the user's instruction, no fresh independent website recheck was performed while completing this previously drafted run. Observation dates reflect the October 19 draft, not claimed publication dates.

No independently verified qualifying Tier 1, Tier 2, or Tier 3 match was established within the examined source sample. The result does not establish universal absence, exclusivity, infringement, misconduct, or that unexamined organizations lack corresponding features.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

**🔴 Tier 1:** A complete corresponding integrated ecosystem independently verified within one identifiable outside organization or system.

**🟡 Tier 2:** Two or more complete distinctive components independently verified as integrated within one identifiable outside system.

**🟣 Tier 3:** One complete distinctive component independently verified within one identifiable outside system.

**🟢 Green:** Common market functionality, partial similarity, or evidence insufficient to establish a qualifying Tier.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

**Observation date:** October 20, 2026. These are source observations
made today; their features and publications may predate this review. The
nine entries below are bounded first-party public-source observations,
not a comprehensive audit of the internet or all apps, backlinks and
repositories.

**🌐 BOT 1 — Websites, Search Engines and SEO.** Practera's official
site documents live employer briefs, university collaboration,
project-based learning, mentoring and managed or self-service program
delivery. The employer-facing project submission page describes outreach
to businesses for real-world research and strategy projects. Guild's
official product page documents employer education benefits. This sample
establishes relevant indexed website material, not a comprehensive
search-ranking, backlink or sitemap audit. Evidence **037, 039, 042,
045**.

**💻 BOT 2 — Applications, Software and Technology.** Practera documents
customizable experiential workflows, learner/mentor feedback, LMS
integration, AI analytics and a mobile-first interface. Symba describes
internship onboarding, project and feedback management, and dashboards.
Public Practera-linked repositories document an Ionic/Angular app and an
MCP/GraphQL integration. Evidence **038, 041, 043, 044**.

**📣 BOT 3 — Social Media, Backlinks and Marketing.** Practera's public
LinkedIn company profile promotes real industry experiences, university
partnerships, employability and program results. Its employer-facing
website invites organizations to submit briefs. Social-post authorship
and site marketing are observed, but a systematic external backlink
inventory, social reach verification and independent analytics were not
performed. Evidence **040, 045**.

**🎓 BOT 4 — Education, Experiential, Tuition and Benefits.** Practera
offers work-integrated and work-based learning with real clients,
feedback, educator oversight and portfolio deliverables; its educator
page also lists simulated learning as a separate program type. Guild
describes employer-funded education, coaching and career mobility. None
of the sampled materials verifies RIAH's precise Apprentice-to-Executive
durations, separate human capstone panel and reimbursement percentages
as one outside system. Evidence **037–039, 041–042, 045**.

**📂 BOT 5 — GitHub, Documentation and Contributors.** The public
intersective/practera-app repository's README identifies Angular, Ionic,
installation and release notes; its GitHub API record lists MIT
licensing and a September 30, 2026 latest returned commit. The
practera-mcp-server README describes assessment review, project
authoring and API integrations. Public code and repository activity are
evidence of those projects, not of RIAH source access, code derivation
or infringement. Evidence **043–044**.

**Evidence accounting:** Nine new records **RIAH-LEGACY-2026-037 through
045**. Existing evidence records **001–036** retain their original dates
and content. **Cumulative register after approval: 45 records**. The
report compares a limited, identifiable source sample; no independent
source established a complete qualifying RIAH-specific Tier 1, Tier 2 or
Tier 3 match. The conclusion does not establish universal absence,
exclusivity, copying, infringement, unlawful access or wrongdoing.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

**🔴 Tier 1:** A complete corresponding integrated ecosystem
independently verified within one identifiable outside organization or
system.

**🟡 Tier 2:** Two or more complete distinctive components independently
verified as integrated within one identifiable outside system.

**🟣 Tier 3:** One complete distinctive component independently verified
within one identifiable outside system.

**🟢 Green:** Common market functionality, partial similarity, or
evidence insufficient to establish a qualifying Tier.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

October 21, 2026: Five first-party public sources were examined across websites/SEO, software, marketing, education/benefits and GitHub/documentation. These are newly observed sources, not verified newly released features. No comprehensive search ranking, backlink audit, app-binary examination, source-code equivalence test or contributor identity audit was performed.

- [Practera](https://practera.com/what-is-practera/): Industry projects, authoring, mentor applications, feedback and analytics.
- [Practera 2026 outreach](https://go.practera.com/imagineif2026): 2026 employer-project marketing and educational partnerships.
- [Symba SHRM21](https://symba.io/symba-at-shrm-2021/): Historical internship management and curriculum marketing.
- [Guild employee benefits](https://guild.com/careers/benefits): Education benefits for employees.
- [Practera SDK README](https://github.com/intersective/practera-sdk-ionic/blob/master/README.md): Public Ionic and Angular SDK development documentation.

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

October 22 retrospective report, with actual source observations on October 23, 2026. Observations are not claimed as October 22 product launches.

- 🌐 BOT 1 — [Canvas Career](https://www.instructure.com/canvas/canvas-career): Public career-aligned skills learning and analytics; comprehensive search ranking and SEO audit not completed.
- 💻 BOT 2 — [Open edX](https://github.com/openedx/openedx-platform): Public LMS and course authoring; historical source revisited.
- 📣 BOT 3 — [Canvas Career announcement](https://www.instructure.com/press-release/instructure-launches-canvas-career-skills-first-ai-powered-learning-experience): Historical workforce-development promotion; comprehensive backlink/social audit not completed.
- 🎓 BOT 4 — [Registered Apprenticeship](https://www.apprenticeship.gov/employers/registered-apprenticeship-program): Paid workplace experience, mentorship, instruction and credentials; historical source revisited.
- 📂 BOT 5 — [Kolibri](https://github.com/learningequality/kolibri): Public offline-first educational software and contribution documentation.

The examined sources do not independently establish the complete integrated RIAH Pathway model. Preserved comparison checks and crosses are historical reference-model comparisons, not fresh verification of all 150 market categories. No copying, infringement or misconduct is inferred.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

Five 🟢 Green observations; 🔴 Tier 1: 0; 🟡 Tier 2: 0; 🟣 Tier 3: 0. Findings apply only to examined sources.

## IX. 🔍 PUBLIC EVIDENCE REVIEW

October 23, 2026: Five first-party public sources were examined across websites, technology, marketing, education/benefits and GitHub. Some sources and features were publicly documented before October 23; they are new observations in this daily register, not claims of newly launched features. No comprehensive SEO ranking, social engagement, backlink, software binary, source-code equivalence or contributor identity audit was performed.

- 🌐 **BOT 1 — Websites / SEO:** [Riipen](https://www.riipen.com/) publicly documents employer–educator–learner project matching and real industry projects. The sampled site supports a project-based work-learning overlap, not the complete RIAH structure.
- 💻 **BOT 2 — Applications / Technology:** [Symba](https://symba.io/sponsor-internship-program-resources/) publicly documents intern onboarding, project administration, feedback loops and analytics. The sampled features do not independently establish RIAH's full human review and six-level system.
- 📣 **BOT 3 — Social Media / Marketing:** [Riipen September 2026 webinar recap](https://www.riipen.com/blog/2026-webinar-recap-between-commitment-and-capacity-five-moves-institutional-leaders-can-make-now) documents public outreach on scaling career-connected learning. Publication preceded the observation date; backlink and social analytics were not independently measured.
- 🎓 **BOT 4 — Education / Benefits:** [Guild FAQ](https://guild.com/faq) documents employer education benefits, learning partners and upfront tuition assistance. This is not evidence of the exact RIAH tuition, work-linked and reimbursement combination.
- 📂 **BOT 5 — GitHub / Contributors:** [Moodle](https://github.com/moodle/moodle) documents an open-source learning management system, documentation and contribution mechanisms. Public repository availability does not imply copying or access to RIAH's source code.

The preserved 150-category matrix reflects historical reference-model comparisons, not fresh verification of every market category. No sampled source independently establishes the complete RIAH Pathway integrated architecture. Ordinary similarity is not evidence of copying, infringement or misconduct.

## X. ⚖️ TIER CLASSIFICATION

Five 🟢 Green observations; 🔴 Tier 1: 0; 🟡 Tier 2: 0; 🟣 Tier 3: 0. Findings concern examined sources only, not universal absence, uniqueness or wrongdoing.


👑 **RIAH Pathway.**
